The rest of this entry is retained verbatim as originally written, for provenance of what was observed and how it was originally (incorrectly) interpreted as a defect. ### Resolution (targeted re-review) Per caller-supplied `acceptance-impact-scope.md`, this finding was independently re-examined against the literal approved contract rather than against this tester&#39;s own unstated usability expectation: - `openspec/changes/create-and-scene/design.md:100` — approved design decision, verbatim: &quot;keys do not hijack focused controls.&quot; No qualification limiting this to keys with &quot;native meaningful effect&quot; appears in the design document. - `openspec/changes/create-and-scene/specs/evolving-scene-presentations/spec.md`, Scenario &quot;Controls keep their keys&quot; — unqualified by key type or control type. - `src/presentation-kit/presentation.test.tsx:62-69` — a committed regression test, `does not hijack navigation keys from a focused control`, that focuses the &quot;Go to step 2: Middle&quot; progress button (a plain `&lt;button&gt;` — the exact element type this finding flagged) and asserts `ArrowRight` does **not** advance `data-step-index`. That reading was this tester&#39;s own preference, not a requirement of the approved artifacts.
